# Content trace: South Korea and the Allied Industrial Base

Each claim of the brief with the dashboard field it comes from, the source datasets and the evidence quality it rests on.

**s1.2** It holds 51% of world exports or production of cathode materials and precursors.

- `aib.contributes.cathode-materials-and-precursors.share` = 51% | AIB strategic.countries[].slots and items | Allied Industrial Base model: roles, compacts, trade restored and new capacity across the allied coalition, computed by the Explorer's authors | United Nations, UN Comtrade Database, HS6 bilateral trade (all reporters); reconciled by the authors | evidence: AIB model run

**s1.3** It is the largest allied holder of battery components and of gas turbines and LNG plant, and it is the best-placed trusted partner of 56 economies for at least one step.

- `aib.recommended_economies` = 56 | posture core cells.p1 (best-placed trusted partner of every economy) | Country strategy choices by technology and supply-chain step (supply-chain need, Strategic Industrial Value, Competitive Position, deployment need, policy alignment), computed by the Explorer's authors | evidence: AIB model run
